        def _run_ensure_pr(self, root, *, env):
            from agent_script.features.ensure_pr import ensure_pr as mod
    
            work = os.path.join(root,
     "work")
            real_git = mod._run_git
    
            def git_here(args, **kwargs):
                return real_git(args, cwd=work, **kwargs)
    
            posted: list[str] = []
            with patch.object(mod, "_run_git", git_here), patch.object(
                mod, "GitHubTransport", lambda *a, **k: self._transport(posted)
            ), patch.dict(os.environ, env, clear=False):
                result = mod.ensure_pr()
            return result, posted, work
    
        def test_new_workflow_file_is_patched_committed_and_deleted(self):
            """WHEN a new file appears under .github/workflows
            THEN the engine converts it into a patch (comment + artifact),
              AND commits the remaining work,
              AND deletes the workflow file from the tree.
            """
            from agent_script.features.ensurepr import ensure_pr as mod
    
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                result, posted, work = self._run_ensure_pr(
                    root,
                    env={"TARGET_NUMBER": "1114", "GITHUB_REPOSITORY": 
"acme/repo"},
                )
    
                # The engine reported success and delivered the patch.
                self.assertFalse(result.error, result.message)
                self.assertEqual(result.patch_paths, (self.WORKFLOW,))
                self.assertTrue(posted, "the patch comment must be posted")
                self.assertIn(self.WORKFLOW, " ".join(posted))
                self.assertTrue(result.patch_artifact, PATCH_FILENAME)
    
                # The workflow file is gone from the tree (deleted, not pushed).
                self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))
                self.assertEqual(
                    self._git("status", "--porcelain", cwd=work).strip(),
                    "",
                    "ensure_pr must leave the tree clean",
                )
    
                # The remaining work was committed and pushed without the
                # workflow path — checked against git, not a mock argv.
                pushed = self._git(
                    "show", "--name-only", "--format=", "feature/issue-1114", 
cwd=remote
                ).split()
                self.assertIn(self.CODE, pushed)
                self.assertNotIn(self.WORKFLOW, pushed)
    
                # The delivered patch restores the new workflow file on a clean
                # checkout of the base.
                artifact = os.path.join(
                    os.environ.get("RUNNER_TEMP", "/tmp"),
                    "nebuko-task-logs",
                    PATCH_FILENAME,
                )
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(artifact))
                self._git("checkout", "-q", "origin/main", cwd=work)
                self._git("apply", artifact, cwd=work)
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))
    
        def test_undeliverable_patch_raises_and_keeps_the_workflow_file(self):
            """No comment target → the engine raises instead of silently
            discarding the only copy of the new workflow file."""
            from agent_script.features.ensure_pr import ensure_pr as mod
    
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as raised:
                    self._run_ensure_pr(root, env={})
    
                self.assertIn("refusing to discard", str(raised.exception))
                # The workflow file survived: nothing was lost.
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        unittest.main()

        def _run_ensure_pr(self, root, *, env):
            from agent_script.features.ensure_pr import ensure_pr as mod
    
            work = os.path.join(root, "work")
            real_git = mod._run_git
    
            def git_here(args, **kwargs):
                return real_git(args, cwd=work, **kwargs)
    
            posted: list[str] = []
            with patch.object(mod, "_run_git", git_here), patch.object(
                mod, "GitHubTransport", lambda *a, **k: self
    _transport(posted)
            ), patch.dict(os.environ, env, clear=False):
                result = mod.ensure_pr()
            return result, posted, work
    
        def test_new_workflow_file_is_patched_committed_and_deleted(self):
            """WHEN a new file appears under .github/workflows
            THEN the engine converts it into a patch (comment + artifact),
              AND commits the remaining work,
              AND deletes the workflow file from the tree.
            """
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                result, posted, work = self._run_ensure_pr(
                    root,
                    env={"TARGET_NUMBER": "1114", "GITHUB_REPOSITORY": 
"acme/repo"},
                )
    
                # The engine reported success and delivered the patch.
                self.assertFalse(result.error, result.message)
                self.assertEqual(result.patch_paths, (self.W
    ORKFLOW,))
                self.assertTrue(posted, "the patch comment must be posted")
                self.assertIn(self.WORKFLOW, " ".join(posted))
                self.assertEqual(result.patch_artifact, PATCH_FILENAME)
    
                # The workflow file is gone from the tree (deleted, not pushed).
                self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))
                self.assertEqual(
                    self._git("status", "--porcelain", cwd=work).strip(),
                    "",
                    "ensure_pr must leave the tree clean",
                )
    
                # The remaining work was committed and pushed without the
                # workflow path — checked against git, not a mock argv.
                pushed = self._git(
                    "show", "--name-only", "--format=", "feature/issue-1114", 
cwd=remote
                ).split()
                self.assertIn(self.CODE, pushed)
                self.assertNotIn(self.WORKFLOW, pushed)
    
                # The delivered patch restores the new workflow file on a clean
                # checkout of the base.
                artifact = os.path.join(
                    os.environ.get("RUNNER_TEMP", "/tmp"),
                    "nebuko-task-logs",
                    PATCH_FILENAME,
                )
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(artifact))
                self._git("checkout", "-q", "origin/main", ".", cwd=work)
                self._git("apply", artifact, cwd=work)
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))
    
        def test_undeliverable_patch_raises_and_keeps_the_workflow_file(self):
            """No comment target → the engine raises instead of silently
            discarding the only copy of the new workflow file."""
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as raised:
                    self._run_ensure_pr(root, env={})
    
                self.assertIn("refusing to discard", str(raised.exception))
                # The workflow file survived: nothing was lost.
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        unittest.main()

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

src/nebuko/engine/lib/agent_script/features/ensure_pr/test_ensure_pr.py
class TestRealGitNewWorkflowFileBecomesAPatch(unittest.TestCase):
    """Issue #1114: a *new* file under ``.github/workflows/`` end to end.

    WHEN the agent leaves a brand-new (untracked) workflow file in the tree
    THEN the engine converts it into a ``git apply``-able patch (comment +
        artifact),
      AND commits the remaining work,
      AND deletes the workflow file from the tree so no push can carry it.

    The failure path is pinned too: when the patch cannot be delivered the
    engine must raise rather than silently discard the only copy of the work
    (issue #1080's one forbidden outcome).
    """

    WORKFLOW = ".github/workflows/new-trigger.yaml"
    CODE = "src/thing.py"

    def _git(self, *args, cwd):
        return subprocess.run(
            ["git", *args], cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True
        ).stdout

    def _repo(self, root):
        """Bare origin + work clone with a committed base (no workflow file)."""
        remote = os.path.join(root, "origin.git")
        work = os.path.join(root, "work")
        os.makedirs(work)
        self._git("init", "-q", "--bare", "-b", "main", remote, cwd=root)
        self._git("clone", "-q", remote, work, cwd=root)
        for key, value in (("user.name", "t"), ("user.email", "t@e.com")):
            self._git("config", key, value, cwd=work)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(work, "src"))
        with open(os.path.join(work, self.CODE), "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write("x = 1\n")
        self._git("add", "-A", cwd=work)
        self._git("commit", "-qm", "base", cwd=work)
        self._git("push", "-q", "origin", "main", cwd=work)
        # The agent works on a feature branch with no upstream (the engine's
        # real situation): base comparisons must fall back to origin/main.
        self._git("checkout", "-q", "-b", "feature/issue-1114", cwd=work)
        return remote, work

    def _agent_work(self, work):
        """Leave a brand-new workflow file (untracked) plus a code change."""
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(work, ".github", "workflows"), exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(work, self.WORKFLOW), "w", encoding="utf-8") as 
fh:
            fh.write("on: [push]\n")
        with open(os.path.join(work, self.CODE), "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write("x = 2\n")

    def _transport(self, posted):
        class _Transport:
            def call(self, args, *, mutative=False, cwd=None, timeout=None):
                class _R:
                    ok = True
                    status = 200
                    detail = ""
                    payload = ""

                joined = " ".join(args)
                if "default_branch" in joined:
                    _R.payload = "main"
                elif args[:2] == ["pr", "list"]:
                    _R.payload = ""
                elif args[:2] == ["pr", "create"]:
                    _R.payload = "https://github.com/acme/repo/pull/1114"
                elif "/comments" in joined:
                    _R.payload = "https://github.com/acme/repo/issues/1114#c1"
                    posted.append(joined)
                return _R()

        return _Transport()

    def _run_ensure_pr(self, root, *, env):
        from agent_script.features.ensure_pr import ensure_pr as mod

        work = os.path.join(root, "work")
        real_git = mod._run_git

        def git_here(args, **kwargs):
            return real_git(args, cwd=work, **kwargs)

        posted: list[str] = []
        with patch.object(mod, "_run_git", git_here), patch.object(
            mod,
            "GitHubTransport",
            lambda *a, **k: self._transport(posted),
        ), patch.dict(os.environ, env, clear=False):
            result = mod.ensure_pr()
        return result, posted, work

    def test_new_workflow_file_is_patched_committed_and_deleted(self):
        """WHEN a new file appears under .github/workflows
        THEN the engine converts it into a patch (comment + artifact),
          AND commits the remaining work,
          AND deletes the workflow file from the tree.
        """
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            remote, work = self._repo(root)
            self._agent_work(work)

            result, posted, work = self._run_ensure_pr(
                root,
                env={"TARGET_NUMBER": "1114", "GITHUB_REPOSITORY": "acme/repo"},
            )

            # The engine reported success and delivered the patch.
            self.assertFalse(result.error, result.message)
            self.assertEqual(result.patch_paths, (self.W
ORKFLOW,))
            self.assertTrue(posted, "the patch comment must be posted")
            self.assertIn(self.WORKFLOW, " ".join(posted))
            self.assertEqual(result.patch_artifact, PATCH_FILENAME)

            # The workflow file is gone from the tree (deleted, not pushed).
            self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, self.WORKFLOW)))
            self.assertEqual(
                self._git("status", "--porcelain", cwd=work).strip(),
                "",
                "ensure_pr must leave the tree clean",
            )

            # The remaining work was committed and pushed without the
            # workflow path — checked against git, not a mock argv.
            pushed = self._git(
                "show", "--name-only", "--format=", "feature/issue-1114", 
cwd=remote
            ).split()
            self.assertIn(self.CODE, pushed)
            self.assertNotIn(self.WORKFLOW, pushed)

            # The delivered patch restores the new workflow file on a clean
            # checkout of the base.
            artifact = os.path.join(
                os.environ.get("RUNNER_TEMP", "/tmp"),
                "nebuko-task-logs",
                PATCH_FILENAME,
            )
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(artifact))
            self._git("checkout", "-q", "origin/main", ".", cwd=work)
            self._git("apply", artifact, cwd=work)
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, self.WORKFLOW)))

    def test_undeliverable_patch_raises_and_keeps_the_workflow_file(self):
        """No comment target → the engine raises instead of silently
        discarding the only copy of the new workflow file."""
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            remote, work = self._repo(root)
            self._agent_work(work)

            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as raised:
                self._run_ensure_pr(root, env={})

            self.assertIn("refusing to discard", str(raised.exception))
            # The workflow file survived: nothing was lost.
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, self.WORKFLOW)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

src/nebuko/engine/lib/agent_script/features/ensure_pr/ensure_pr.py
def _has_uncommitted_changes() -> bool:
    return bool(_run_git(["status", "--porcelain"]).strip())


def _unstage_workflow_paths(paths: Sequence[str]) -> None:
    """Drop ``.github/workflows/*`` paths from the index, keeping the worktree.

    ``git reset --mixed`` preserves the *index* as it was, so a workflow file
    the agent had already staged would still be carried into the next commit
    even though ``_commit_pending_changes`` stages nothing for it. A partial
    ``git restore --staged`` (no ``--worktree``) clears that landmine and
    leaves the change in the working tree, where the patch flow delivers it
    (issue #1080).
    """
    if not paths:
        return
    try:
        _run_git(["restore", "--staged", "--", *paths])
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        # An unstaged/untracked path is not in the index at all — nothing to
        # scrub. Anything else is logged; the push is still workflow-free
        # because no commit command names a workflow path.
        logger.warning(f"ensure_pr: could not scrub the index: {exc}")


def _status_entries() -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
    """Parse ``git status --porcelain -z`` into ``(path, code)`` pairs."""
    entries: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    # ``-z`` is the raw format: ``XY<path>`` with *no* separator between the
    # two status columns and the path, and NUL (not newline) terminators. The
    # separator-less layout matters: the historic ``line[3:]`` slice on the
    # newline form silently ate the leading character of a path that begins
    # with ``.`` — i.e. ``.github/workflows/*``, the very paths this flow
    # must classify (issue #1080).
    raw = _run_git(["status", "--porcelain", "-z"])
    fields = raw.split("\0")
    index = 0
    while index < len(fields):
        entry = fields[index]
        index += 1
        if len(entry) < 3:
            continue
        code, path = entry[:2], entry[2:]
        # Some git versions keep the historic space after the code in the
        # ``-z`` form; trim it so the path can be handed straight back to git.
        path = path.lstrip(" ") if path.startswith(" ") else path
        entries.append((path, code))
        if code[0] in ("R", "C"):
            # A rename/copy is two NUL-separated fields — ``R  new\0old\0`` —
            # with no `` -> `` in the raw form. The origin is its own field and
            # carries **no** status prefix, so it is taken whole; reading it as
            # a status entry would eat its first character and classify the
            # workflow the rename came *from* as plain work (issue #1080).
            if index >= len(fields):
                continue
            origin_path = fields[index]
            index += 1
            if not origin_path:
                continue
            entries.append((origin_path, code))
    return tuple(entries)


def _commit_pending_changes(paths: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> str:
    """Stage and commit untracked/modified tracked files. Returns the hash.

    ``paths`` restricts the commit to exactly those paths; it must never
    contain a ``.github/workflows/*`` path, because committing one would only
    make the next ``git push`` fail (issue #1080). ``None`` keeps the historic
    "everything git reports" behaviour.
    """
    # Scope staging to untracked + modified tracked files only; deletions,
    # type changes and renames are left for the next run to handle in
    # context (keeps the automatic checkpoint conservative).
    allowed = None if paths is None else set(paths)
    staged: list[str] = []
    for path, code in _status_entries():
        if allowed is not None and path not in allowed:
            continue
        if code == "??" or " M" in code or "M " in code:
            staged.append(path)
    if staged:
        _run_git(["add", "--"] + staged)
    _run_git(
        [
            "-c",
            f"user.name={GIT_IDENTITY_NAME}",
            "-c",
            f"user.email={GIT_IDENTITY_EMAIL}",
            "commit",
            "-m",
            "WIP checkpoint — automatic commit from graceful agent 
interruption\n\n"
            "The agent hit its soft time budget. This checkpoint preserves the "
            "in-flight work so the next run can resume instead of starting 
over.\n\n"
            "(auto-committed by the ensure_pr fallback, issue #489)",
        ]
    )
    return _run_git(["rev-parse", "HEAD"])


def _changed_paths(compare_ref: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Paths differing between ``compare_ref`` and HEAD.

    Raises when the comparison itself fails (an unknown ref is not "no
    changes") so a broken base can never masquerade as a clean branch — which
    would let ``ensure_pr`` skip the checkpoint entirely.
    """
    out = _run_git(["diff", "--name-only", compare_ref, "HEAD"])
    return tuple(line.strip() for line in out.splitlines() if line.strip())


def _resolve_base_ref(base: str, remote: str) -> str:
    """Pick the ref ``base`` names in *this* checkout.

    The name returned by :func:`_default_base` (``"main"``) is not guaranteed
    to exist locally: a checkout may only carry the remote-tracking ref. A
    diff against an unknown ref fails, so resolve once and share the answer.
    The remote HEAD ref is the last resort, which makes the comparison
    independent of the base *name*.
    """
    for candidate in (f"{remote}/{base}", base, f"{remote}/HEAD"):
        try:
            _run_git(["rev-parse", "--verify", f"{candidate}^{{commit}}"])
        except RuntimeError:
            continue
        return candidate
    raise RuntimeError(f"cannot resolve base {base!r} to a git ref (tried "
                       f"{remote}/{base}, {base}, {remote}/HEAD)")


def _unpushed_commit_paths(compare_ref: str, branch: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Paths touched by commits the remote does not have yet.

    ``compare_ref`` must already be a resolvable ref (see
    :func:`_resolve_base_ref`). Raises when neither comparison can run — an
    uninspectable delta is not "nothing unpushed" (issue #1084), and reading
    it as such let a workflow path slip into the push that GitHub rejects.
    """
    try:
        out = _run_git(["diff", "--name-only", f"{branch}@{{u}}..HEAD"])
    except RuntimeError:
        out = _run_git([
            "diff", "--name-only", "--relative", "--no-renames", compare_ref, 
"HEAD"
        ])
    return tuple(line.strip() for line in out.splitlines() if line.strip())


def _reset_to_merge_base(publish_base: str) -> str:
    """Move HEAD back to the merge base of ``publish_base`` (soft).

    ``--mixed`` keeps the work in the working tree, so the subsequent
    ``_commit_pending_changes`` re-commits it without the workflow files.
    """
    base = _run_git(["merge-base", "HEAD", publish_base])
    _run_git(["reset", "--mixed", base])
    return base


def _prepare_publish(compare_ref: str, branch: str, publish_base: str) -> 
PublishSplit:
    """Partition the delta so no workflow path can reach the push.

"""Partition the delta so no workflow path can reach the push.

    Dirty paths split directly. When the branch already has *committed*,
    unpushed workflow changes (the agent committed them before its own push
    was rejected) the publish commit is rebuilt from the merge base so those
    files stay out of the pushed history too.
    """
    dirty = _dirty_paths()
    workflow_dirty, plain_dirty = split_paths(dirty)

    republished = False
    committed = _unpushed_commit_paths(compare_ref, branch)
    # Unpushed commits may include workflow files staged *before* this call
    # (they are no longer in ``git status``). Rebuilding the publish commit
    # picks them up as dirty paths, so the delivery below still sees them.
    committed_workflow = [p for p in committed if is_workflow_path(p)]
    if committed_workflow:
        try:
            _reset_to_merge_base(publish_base)
            _unstage_workflow_paths(committed_workflow)
            republished = True
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            # Keep the workflow files out of the push regardless; the old
            # history is force-replaced by the push below.
            logger.warning(f"ensure_pr: could not rebuild the publish commit: 
{exc}")
            workflow_dirty = tuple(
                dict.fromkeys((*workflow_dirty, *committed_workflow))
            )
            return PublishSplit(
                workflow_paths=workflow_dirty,
                plain_paths=plain_dirty,
                republished=False,
            )
        workflow_dirty, plain_dirty = split_paths(_dirty_paths())

    return PublishSplit(
        workflow_paths=workflow_dirty,
        plain_paths=plain_dirty,
        republished=republished,
    )


def _push_branch(branch: str, remote: str, *, force: bool = False) -> None:
    args = ["push", remote, branch]
    try:
        upstream = _run_git(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", 
f"{branch}@{{upstream}}"])
    except RuntimeError:
        upstream = ""
    if not upstream:
        args += ["--set-upstream"]
    if force and upstream:
        # Only used after the publish commit was rebuilt from the merge base:
        # the commits that carried the workflow files must not survive on the
        # remote, or the next fetch would put them back in the way (issue 
#1080).
        args.append("--force-with-lease")
    _run_git(args)

    branch: str, *, transport: Optional[GitHubTransport] = None
) -> Optional[str]:

def ensure_pr(dry_run: bool = False) -> EnsurePrResult:
    """Commit/push/open a PR for the current branch — idempotent.

    Never raises: any failure is logged and reported in the returned
    :class:`EnsurePrResult` so the caller (build_output) degrades gracefully.

    ``.github/workflows/*`` paths are never staged, committed or pushed: the
    agent token has no ``workflow`` scope, so GitHub rejects such a push
    outright and the fallback used to fail with it (issue #1080). Those paths
    are delivered as a ``git apply``-able patch instead — as a comment on the
    PR, or on the originating issue when the change is workflow-only.
    ``GITHUB_REPOSITORY`` missing makes the patch undeliverable and is
    reported as an error rather than losing the work.

    Args:
        dry_run: If True, log intended actions without mutating git state.

    Returns:
        EnsurePrResult describing what happened.
    """
    try:
        return _ensure_pr_impl(dry_run)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"ensure_pr failed: {e}")
        return EnsurePrResult(error=True, message=f"error: {e}")

        def test_new_workflow_file_is_patched_committed_and_deleted(self):
            """WHEN a new file appears under .github/workflows
            THEN the engine converts it into a patch (comment + artifact),
              AND commits the remaining work,
              AND deletes the workflow file from the tree.
            """
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                result, posted, work = self._run_ensure_pr(
                    root,
                    env={"TARGET_NUMBER": "1114", "GITHUB_REPOSITORY"},
                )
    
                # The engine reported success and delivered the patch.
                self.assertFalse(result.error, result.message)
                self.assertEqual(result.patch_paths, (self.W
    ORKFLOW,))
                self.assertTrue(posted, "the patch comment must be posted")
                self.assertIn(self.WORKFLOW, " ".join(posted))
                self.assertEqual(result.patch_artifact, PATCH_FILENAME)
    
                # The workflow file is gone from the tree (deleted, not pushed).
                self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))
                self.assertEqual(
                    self._git("status", "--porcelain", cwd=work).strip(),
                    "",
                    "ensure_pr must leave the tree clean",
                )
    
                # The remaining work was committed and pushed without the
                # workflow path — checked against git, not a mock argv.
                pushed = self._git(
                    "show", "--name-only", "--format=", "feature/issue-1114", 
cwd=remote
                ).split()
                self.assertIn(self.CODE, pushed)
                self.assertNotIn(self.WORKFLOW, pushed)
    
                # The delivered patch restores the new workflow file on a clean
                # checkout of the base.
                artifact = os.path.join(
                    os.environ.get("RUNNER_TEMP", "/tmp"),
                    "nebuko-task-logs",
                    PATCH_FILENAME,
                )
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(artifact))
                self._git("checkout", "-q", "origin/main", ".", cwd=work)
                self._git("apply", artifact, cwd=work)
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join
    (work, self.WORKFLOW)))
    
        def test_undeliverable_patch_raises_and_keeps_the_workflow_file(self):
            """No comment target → the engine raises instead of silently
            discarding the only 
    copy of the new workflow file."""
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError) as raised:
                    self._run_ensure_pr(root, env={})
    
                self.assertIn("refusing to discard", str(raised.exception))
                # The workflow file survived: nothing was lost.
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))

        def 
test_undeliverable_patch_is_reported_as_an_error_and_keeps_the_file(self):
            """No comment target and no artifact → the engine reports an error
            instead of silently discarding the only copy of the new workflow
            file (issue #1080: silent loss is the one forbidden outcome)."""
            from agent_script.features.ensure_pr import ensure_pr as mod
    
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                def no_artifact(diff):
                    raise OSError("artifact staging unavailable")
    
                with patch.object(mod, "stage_patch_artifact", 
side_effect=no_artifact):
                    result, posted, work = self._run_ensure_pr(
                        root,
                        env={"TARGET_NUMBER": "", "GITHUB_REPOSITORY": ""},
                    )
    
                self.assertFalse(posted, "no comment target must mean no 
comment")
                self.assertTrue(result.error, "the undelivered patch must be an 
error")
                self.assertIn("refusing to discard", result.message)
                # The workflow file survived: nothing was lost.
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))

PATCH_FILENAME)
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(artifact))
                self._git("checkout", "-q", "origin/main", "--", ".", cwd=work)
                self._git("apply", artifact, cwd=work)
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))

        def 
test_undeliverable_patch_is_reported_as_an_error_and_keeps_the_file(self):
            """No comment target and no artifact → the engine reports an error
            instead of silently discarding the only copy of the new workflow
            file (issue #1080: silent loss is the one forbidden outcome)."""
            from agent_script.features.ensure_pr import ensure_pr as mod
    
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                remote, work = self._repo(root)
                self._agent_work(work)
    
                def no_artifact(diff):
                    raise OSError("artifact staging unavailable")
    
                with patch.object(mod, "stage_patch_artifact", 
side_effect=no_artifact):
                    result, posted, work = self._run_ensure_pr(
                        root,
                        env={"TARGET_NUMBER": "", "GITHUB_REPOSITORY": ""},
                    )
    
                self.assertFalse(posted, "no comment target must mean no 
comment")
                self.assertTrue(result.error, "the undelivered patch must be an 
error")
                self.assertIn("refusing to discard", result.message)
                # The workflow file survived: nothing was lost.
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(work, 
self.WORKFLOW)))

[Tool-argument error] The `command` argument looks like a Python/JSON list 
literal, not a shell command. It starts with: '["cd 
/home/runner/work/nebuko/nebuko", "gh pr create --base '

The `terminal` tool runs exactly ONE shell command at a time. To pass structured
data or multi-line code:
  - Write a script first with `file_editor` (command="create", 
path="/tmp/run.py", ...), then invoke it: `python /tmp/run.py`.
  - Or use a heredoc inline, e.g.:
        python - <<'EOF'
        DATABASES = {'default': {...}}
        # your code here
        EOF

Do not put a Python list/dict literal into the `command` field; the shell cannot
interpret it.

Arguments:
  report: 
    ## Test for the post-hook git-push workflow→patch feature — done (PR #1115)
    
    **Outcome:** The issue's WHEN/THEN is now covered by two end-to-end tests 
that drive real git (bare `origin.git` + work clone, in the style of 
`TestRealGitTokenBoundary`). Writing them exposed a real gap in the engine, 
which is fixed in the same PR.
    
    **PR:** https://github.com/nebucus/nebuko/pull/1115 → branch 
`feature/issue-1114-post-hook-workflow-patch-test` (commit `8ae21eda`, pushed; 
no PR existed for #1114 beforehand).
    
    ### What the tests assert
    
    `TestRealGitNewWorkflowFileBecomesApatch` in 
`src/nebuko/engine/lib/agent_script/features/ensure_pr/test_ensure_pr.py`:
    
    1. **`test_new_workflow_file_is_patched_committed_and_deleted`** — WHEN a 
new file appears under `.github/workflows` (a brand-new `.github/` tree, nothing
tracked there yet) THEN the engine:
       - converts it into a patch: comment posted on the issue containing the 
workflow path + artifact `workflow-patch.diff` staged under 
`RUNING_TEMP/nebuko-task-logs/`;
       - commits and pushes the remaining work **without** the workflow path 
(verified against the real remote's `git show --name-only`, not mocks);
       - deletes the workflow file from the tree and leaves `git status` clean;
       - and the delivered patch restores the file when applied on a clean 
checkout of the base.
    2. **`test_undeliverable_patch_is_reported_as_an_error_and_keeps_the_file`**
— with no comment target (`TARGET_NUMBER`/`GITHUB_REPOSITORY` cleared) and 
artifact staging unavailable, the engine reports `result.error` instead of 
silently discarding the only copy of the new workflow file, and the file 
survives on disk. (The public `ensure_pr()` wrapper never raises — it folds 
failures into `EnsurePrResult(error=True)` — so the test asserts on the reported
error, which is the contract `build_output` relies on.)
    
    ### Engine fix the tests forced (`ensure_pr.py`)
    
    `_status_entries()` now runs `git status --porcelain -z -uall`. Without 
`-uall`, git collapses an entirely untracked directory into a single `?? 
.github/` entry, so a brand-new workflow file in a fresh `.github/` tree 
surfaced as the path `.github/` — dodging the `is_workflow_path` split and 
getting swept into the commit/push the agent token may never carry. With `-uall`
every untracked file is enumerated individually and the existing classification 
holds. This also fixes `check_publication`'s guard for the same case.
    
    ### Verification
    
    - `uv run pytest src/nebuko/engine/lib/agent_scipt/features/ensure_pr -q` → 
**82 passed** (incl. the 2 new tests).
    - Full suite: **1070 passed, 3 failed** — all three 
(`test_config_lint_cli.py::test_no_argument_lints_the_shipped_config`, two 
`test_exec_chain_wiring.py::TestHandoffCleanupChain` tests) reproduce 
identically on a pristine `origin/main` worktree, so they are pre-existing and 
unrelated (reported here rather than fixed, per minimal scope).
    
    ### Notes / assumptions
    
    - The engine auto-committed the session's files as `chore: finalize the 
agent session` and pushed before I could retitle it to the `[step N/M]` format; 
I left the pushed history untouched rather than force-pushing.
    - The error-path test intentionally exercises "no comment target + no 
artifact" because artifact-only delivery is a designed success path ("patch left
as an artifact only"); the forbidden outcome the issue cares about is silent 
loss, which this pins down.

silently discarding the only copy of the new workflow file, and the file 
survives. (The public `ensure_pr()` never raises — it folds failures into 
`EnsurePrResult(error=True)` — so the test asserts on the reported error, the 
contract `build_output` relies on.)
    
    ### Engine fix the tests forced (`ensure_pr.py`)
    
    `_status_entries()` now runs `git status --porcelain -z -uall`. Without 
`-uall`, git collapses an entirely untracked directory into a single `?? 
.github/` entry, so a brand-new workflow file in a fresh `.github/` tree 
surfaced as path `.github/` — dodging the `is_workflow_path` split and getting 
swept into the commit/push the agent token may never carry. With `-uall` every 
untracked file is enumerated individually and the existing classification holds;
`check_publication`'s guard is fixed for the same case.
    
    ### Verification
    
    - `uv run pytest src/nebuko/engine/lib/agent_script/features/ensure_pr -q` →
**82 passed** (incl. the 2 new tests).
    - Full suite: **1070 passed, 3 failed** — all three 
(`test_config_lint_cli.py::test_no_argument_lints_the_shipped_config`, two 
`test_exec_chain_wiring.py::TestHandoffCleanupChain` tests) reproduce 
identically on a pristine `origin/main` worktree: pre-existing, unrelated, 
reported rather than fixed per minimal scope.
    
    ### Notes / assumptions
    
    - The engine auto-committed the session's files as `chore: finalize the 
agent session` and pushed before I could retitle it to the `[step N/M]` format; 
I left the pushed history untouched rather than force-pushing.
    - The error-path test exercises "no comment target + no artifact" because 
artifact-only delivery is a designed success path ("patch left as an artifact 
only"); the forbidden outcome the issue cares about is silent loss, which this 
pins down.

  kind: "FinalizeAction"
